### Dialogue

- **AUSTRIA → TURKEY**: BUL is unowned and adjacent to my F GRE and A SER — I intend to take it this spring. I'd prefer we agree now: BUL goes to me, and I won't press further east toward CON or SMY. If you contest BUL, we both lose ground while Russia recovers.

- **AUSTRIA → RUSSIA**: Turkey holds RUM with A RUM and F BLA support — that's a strong position threatening both of us. I'm pushing toward BUL this spring; if you can press RUM from SEV, we can squeeze Turkey from two sides and deny them consolidation in the Balkans.
- **AUSTRIA → GERMANY**: I'm focused on BUL this spring and want to keep my eastern flank secure. How are you handling Russia — are you pressing east or consolidating? I'd like to coordinate so we're not leaving gaps for Russia to exploit between us.
- **ENGLAND → GERMANY**: 1901 went well for both of us — now I'm eyeing Sweden this spring to keep growing north. I'd like to discuss 1902 coordination: if France is your main concern, I can pressure them from the north while you push from the east, and we split the gains. What's your read on the board?
- **ENGLAND → FRANCE**: 1901 was clean for both of us — I'm planning to consolidate in the north this year, not push into your territory. I'd like to keep our non-aggression going: Belgium stays mine, Brest stays yours, and we each look outward for growth rather than at each other.
- **ENGLAND → RUSSIA**: I'm moving toward Sweden this year — I'd appreciate confirmation you're not contesting it, since we agreed NWY was mine and SWE was in your orbit. If you're focused on Turkey or Austria, we can keep Scandinavia clean between us and both grow without friction.
- **FRANCE → ITALY**: I want to coordinate directly this spring — your A TYR is sitting next to MUN, and my A BUR can move there too. If you support A BUR into MUN, we crack Germany open and both benefit enormously from their collapse. Can you commit to A TYR S A BUR - MUN this spring? I'll push A BUR - MUN and we split the gains.
- **FRANCE → GERMANY**: I want to be straight with you: I'm not planning to push into MUN this spring, but I need to know your intentions toward BEL and HOL. If you're expanding north, I'll focus south; if you're pushing west, we should talk about boundaries. What's your move this turn?
- **FRANCE → ENGLAND**: Good work last year — we both grew cleanly. I want to keep the peace on our shared border this spring while I focus southward. Are you planning any moves toward the continent that I should know about, or are you consolidating in the north?
- **GERMANY → RUSSIA**: I'm moving F DEN to SWE this spring — we bounced last fall and I'd rather we avoid that again. I'm open to a deal: SWE for me, and I'll support your position in the Baltic or stay out of your affairs in the north. Let's not waste both our moves on a bounce when there are bigger targets.
- **GERMANY → ENGLAND**: We're both at 5 centers — solid starts. I'm pushing for SWE this spring to lock it in. I'd like to discuss 1902 coordination: are you planning to push further into Scandinavia or consolidate? I want to make sure we don't end up in conflict over the Baltic region.
- **GERMANY → ITALY**: Your A TYR is sitting right next to MUN — I need to know your intentions. If you're moving through on your way east or north, I can live with that, but if you're planning to push into MUN I'll have to respond forcefully. What's your plan for spring 1902?
- **GERMANY → AUSTRIA**: Italy's A TYR is a concern for both of us — it could threaten MUN or BOH depending on where it moves. I'm pushing east with A SIL this spring; if you're holding GAL, we could coordinate to pressure Russia or box in Italy. What are your intentions for 1902?
- **ITALY → AUSTRIA**: I need to grow this year and GRE is the natural next step for me — I want to be honest rather than surprise you. If you're willing to pull F GRE back and focus on the Balkans (RUM, BUL), I'll support your eastern expansion and keep A TYR aimed at Germany rather than TRI or VIE. What would it take to make that work?
- **ITALY → TURKEY**: I'm eyeing GRE this spring with F ION — Austria holds it and I think it's time I pushed there. If you're looking at BUL, we could end up adjacent in the Balkans, which I'd rather handle cooperatively than competitively. Are you open to a division of the Balkans where I take GRE and you hold RUM/BUL?
- **ITALY → FRANCE**: Germany has 5 centers and is well-positioned in the north — they look like the power most likely to run away with the game if England and France stay cautious. I'm pushing into the Mediterranean this year; if you're considering a move against Germany, now is the time before they consolidate further.
- **RUSSIA → AUSTRIA**: Turkey took RUM from me and is consolidating fast — they have RUM, BUL threat, and BLA. I'm pushing F SEV back toward RUM this spring; if you can move into BUL or support pressure on Turkey's Balkan position, we can roll them back together. What are your plans for SER and BUL this year?
- **RUSSIA → GERMANY**: SWE is still unowned after we both bounced last fall — I'm moving F BOT to SWE this spring and I'd ask you not to contest it again. You have HOL, DEN, and a strong position; let me have SWE and I'll stay clear of your Baltic interests.

</details>
